					<div id="contentSub"><div id="mw-content-subtitle"></div></div>
					
					
					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content mw-content-ltr" lang="en" dir="ltr"><div class="mw-parser-output"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none">List of Christian saints from 451 AD</div>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1033289096">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}</style><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">This article is about Christian saints post-451 AD. For Christian saints before this time, see <a href="/wiki/List_of_early_Christian_saints" title="List of early Christian saints">List of early Christian saints</a>. For those whom the Catholic Church has canonized, see <a href="/wiki/List_of_Catholic_saints" title="List of Catholic saints">List of Catholic saints</a>. For other uses, see <a href="/wiki/List_of_saints_(disambiguation)" class="mw-disambig" title="List of saints (disambiguation)">List of saints (disambiguation)</a>.</div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt">
</p><p>This sortable <b>list of Christian <a href="/wiki/Saint" title="Saint">saints</a></b> includes—where known—a surname, location, and personal attribute (or those attributes included as part of the historical name).
</p>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Listed">Listed</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_saints&amp;action=edit&amp;section=1" title="Edit section: Listed">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p>Canonized <a href="/wiki/List_of_Catholic_saints" title="List of Catholic saints">Roman Catholic saints</a> have been through a formal institutional process resulting in their canonization.  There have been thousands of canonizations.  <a href="/wiki/Pope_John_Paul_II" title="Pope John Paul II">Pope John Paul II</a> alone canonized 110 individuals, as well as many group canonizations such as 110 martyr saints of China, 103 Korean martyrs, 117 Vietnamese martyrs, the Mexican Martyrs, Spanish martyrs and French revolutionary martyrs.  Note that 78 popes are considered saints.<sup id="cite_ref-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-1">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup>
</p><p>Among the <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Orthodoxy" title="Eastern Orthodoxy">Eastern Orthodox</a> and <a href="/wiki/Oriental_Orthodoxy" class="mw-redirect" title="Oriental Orthodoxy">Oriental Orthodox</a> Communions, the numbers may be even higher, since there is no fixed process of "<a href="/wiki/Canonization" title="Canonization">canonization</a>" and each individual jurisdiction within the two Orthodox communions independently maintains parallel lists of saints that have only partial overlap.  
</p><p>The <a href="/wiki/Anglican_Communion" title="Anglican Communion">Anglican Communion</a> recognizes pre-<a href="/wiki/Protestant_Reformation" class="mw-redirect" title="Protestant Reformation">Reformation</a> saints, as does the <a href="/wiki/United_Methodist_Church" title="United Methodist Church">United Methodist Church</a>.  Persons who have led lives of celebrated sanctity or missionary zeal are included in the Calendar of the Prayer Book "...without thereby enrolling or commending such persons as saints of the Church..."  Similarly, any individuals commemorated in the <a href="/wiki/Calendar_of_Saints_(Lutheran)" class="mw-redirect" title="Calendar of Saints (Lutheran)">Lutheran calendar of saints</a> will be listed.  Other denominations maintain their own <a href="/wiki/Calendar_of_saints#See_also" title="Calendar of saints">Calendars of Saints</a>.
</p><p><br />
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Christian_saints_since_AD_300">Christian saints since AD 300</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_saints&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Christian saints since AD 300">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table class="wikitable sortable" width="100%">

<tbody><tr>
<th rowspan="2">Saint
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of birth
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of death
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of<br />canonization
</th>
<th colspan="5">Tradition
</th></tr>
<tr>
<th><a href="/wiki/Anglican" class="mw-redirect" title="Anglican">Anglican</a>
</th>
<th><a href="/wiki/Oriental_Orthodox" class="mw-redirect" title="Oriental Orthodox">Oriental Orthodox</a>
</th>
<th><a href="/wiki/Eastern_Orthodox_Church" title="Eastern Orthodox Church">Eastern Orthodox</a>
</th>
<th><a href="/wiki/Roman_Catholic" class="mw-redirect" title="Roman Catholic">Roman Catholic</a>
</th>
<th>Total

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Notes_on_saints_list">Notes on saints list</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_saints&amp;action=edit&amp;section=3" title="Edit section: Notes on saints list">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<p><sup>4</sup> <i>Common Worship</i> has "Commemoration".<br />
<sup>6</sup> <a href="/wiki/Eastern_Rite_Catholic_Churches" class="mw-redirect" title="Eastern Rite Catholic Churches">Eastern Rite Catholic Churches</a> only. <br />
<sup>7</sup> <a href="/wiki/Russian_Orthodox_Church" title="Russian Orthodox Church">Russian Orthodox Church</a> only. <br />
<sup>8</sup><sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="exact citation is needed as to exactly which part, if any, of the Orthodox Church venerates this saint. (February 2016)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup><br />
<sup>9</sup> <a href="/wiki/History_of_Christianity_in_Ukraine#Eastern_Orthodox" title="History of Christianity in Ukraine">Ukrainian Orthodox Church</a>.
</p>
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Catholic_known_canonizations">Catholic known canonizations</span><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=List_of_saints&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Catholic known canonizations">edit</a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></h2>
<table class="wikitable sortable" width="100%">

<tbody><tr>
<th rowspan="2">Saint
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of birth
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of death
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Date of<br />canonization
</th>
<th rowspan="2">Pope reigning
